# TASK_NOTES
## Current request (2026-09-01) — photo → printable product pipeline (design only)

David: "a customer sent me a photo and asked me to make this ... how do we make
stuff our own and functioning correctly" — re: a Ghostface-style candle holder,
plus more products wanted for Darrell's site. Saturn holds the printers, Blender
and the Tripo key.

### Locked decisions (David, 2026-09-01)
- **Parametric fixture + AI skin.** Functional geometry (base, candle cradle,
  hollowing) comes from a Blender Python fixture library computed from params;
  Tripo generates only the decorative shell. Booleaned together in Blender.
  Rejected: post-processing Tripo output alone (candle fit becomes luck), and a
  human-in-the-loop Blender step (does not scale, Saturn can't run it unattended).
- **Design doc first**, before any implementation.

### File shortlist (approved scope — 2026-09-01 pipeline design)
- `docs/plans/2026-09-01-photo-to-printable-pipeline-design.md` (new, write)
- `TASK_NOTES.md` (work-log bullet)
- Read-only for grounding: `backend/services/tripo3d.ts`,
  `backend/services/glb-to-stl.ts`, `backend/routes/print-bridge.ts`,
  `backend/services/etsy-copyright-gate.ts`
- No implementation files edited this session.

- 2026-09-01 — David answered the four open questions; folded into the doc as

  single-colour with a socketed mask part). **PLA ships now**; PETG only for

- 2026-09-01 — **Phase 1 CORE COMPLETE** on `earth/zero-nine/print-factory-core`
  (7 commits, 1ed2ff7..ea69bf4). 25/25 tests green: 20 pure-Python + 5 real
  headless-Blender integration. Real METRICS from the engine for a 20mm cube:
  `volume_mm3 7999.999999999999, grams_est 9.9, manifold true, bbox 20/20/20,
  fits_build_volume true, warnings []` — the metrics contract now demonstrably
  works end to end. Deviations from my own plan, all justified: replaced the
  `_selftest_cube_mm` hack with a dedicated `_selftest` fixture (the plan would
  have put test-only branches inside the production `qr_plaque`, which does not
  exist until Task 7); fixed `mathutils.Vector` imported at the bottom of

  never specified; added Task 6b for KNOWN_FIXTURES/_REGISTRY drift.
- 2026-09-01 — **IMPORTANT, affects the Phase 2 bridge contract: Blender exits 0
  when a `--python` script raises an uncaught exception.** Verified directly on
  5.2.1 — raise → exit 0; `sys.exit(1)` → 1; raise + `--python-exit-code 1` → 1.
  So a crashed prep job (bad spec, boolean failure, OOM) is indistinguishable
  from a successful one, and `assert returncode == 0` can never fail on its own.
  Every caller of `prep.py` — including Saturn over the print bridge — MUST pass
  `--python-exit-code 1` or it will mark failed jobs complete with no artifacts,
  surfacing much later as a missing file instead of a failed job. Fixed +
  committed (ea69bf4) and written into the plan as a Phase 2 requirement.
- 2026-09-01 — **Both product lines shipped real STLs.** QR plaque (`3135175`):
  65.6x65.6x3mm, 14.6g, manifold, and the agent PROVED it is not mirrored by
  re-parsing the exported STL and rebuilding the code from actual pocket depths
  — bit-identical, 541/541 dark. Candle cradles S/M/L (`38985c5`): 232/272/321g,
  all manifold, bores exactly on the ladder. Caveat recorded: the cradle is still
  the bare fixture (a cylinder) — not a sellable product until the shell union.
- 2026-09-01 — **Shell generated for the union, and the provider survey needs
  correcting.** `hunyuan-3d-3.1` FAILED with `ResourceInsufficient` (Tencent's
  own upstream capacity error) — the model I recommended in §16 is currently
  unreliable and must not be the default. `firtoz/trellis` worked but ONLY via
  the versioned endpoint: `POST /v1/models/<owner>/<name>/predictions` 404s for
  community models; they need `POST /v1/predictions` with `{"version": id}`.
  Working route: text -> gpt-image-2 (~$0.04, first try) -> trellis -> GLB.
  **Raw output measured 15,658 non-manifold edges on 39,434 tris** from a clean
  reference image — the hardest evidence yet that voxel_remesh before any
  boolean is load-bearing, not precautionary. The generated wraith has a blank
  featureless face: §7's interpretation path executed, original IP.
- 2026-09-01 — **Publish pipeline built, DRY-RUN ONLY, nothing written to prod**
  (`060f454`, `d38ec41` on `earth/zero-nine/print-factory-publish`). 37/37
  pricing tests (independently re-run by me), full backend suite 567 pass.
  Prices: plaque $9.99, cradles $54.99/$61.99/$70.99 off measured mass + an
  ESTIMATED print time (nothing sliced yet — the layer-count term is ~60% of
  cost on a tall part and is the model's largest error term). Price floor is
  pinned to $8.00 deliberately = `PRICE_BANDS['3d-prints']` in
  `presentation-qa.ts`, so the module cannot price a product that fails ITP's
  own QA gate. Refusal path proven: `--commit` CANNOT bypass the metrics gate.
  Agent added an unrequested STL/metrics pairing check that catches publishing
  one fixture's STL against another's metrics (wrong price, wrong object, no
  error) via exact binary-STL triangle count — genuinely good catch.
- 2026-09-01 — **Three live-schema findings from the publish work, worth acting
  on separately:** (1) **`3d-prints` has NO row in `product_categories`** (only
  dtf-transfers/hoodies/metal-art/shirts/test-category/tumblers) yet 3 live
  products carry `category='3d-prints'` and `presentation-qa.ts:273` +
  `seo.ts:21` + print-bridge + approvals all treat it as real — VERIFIED in code
  by me; `products.category` (text) and `category_id` (FK) have drifted.
  (2) `gcs-storage.ts#uploadFile()` CANNOT write arbitrary paths — it hardcodes
  `users/<userId>/<folder>/<file>` with a closed folder union; use
  `google-cloud-storage.ts#uploadImageFromBuffer(buffer, destPath, contentType)`
  (generic despite the name). Public GCS reads are blocked org-wide, so plain
  storage.googleapis.com URLs 403 — signed URLs required. (3) `products.status`
  has NO CHECK constraint; 'draft'/'pending_approval'/'active' are free text
  from different call sites, and the vendor RLS trigger that pins
  `is_active := false` does NOT apply to service-role scripts — so both flags
  must be set explicitly, never left to defaults.
- 2026-09-01 — **Shell union SHIPPED and works** (7c37ff1, 1cf12cb, c368cd9,
  92e6d28, 4f0cbf6 — 159 tests). Generated an original-IP wraith shell
  (text -> gpt-image-2 -> trellis) and fused it with `candle_cradle`. First
  attempt was a failure worth recording: the figure was NARROWER than the jar it
  had to hold, so the cradle swallowed it — shell contributed 0.26% of the part
  and it rendered as a plain cylinder. Root cause was MY prompt (a tall slender
  standing statue); David's reference is squat and wide, robe flaring PAST the
  jar. Regenerated wide -> 56.5% proud. **Lesson for the pipeline: the shell
  prompt must be constrained by the fixture's dimensions — a figure narrower
  than the vessel cannot survive the union.**
- 2026-09-01 — **Two of my own analyses were wrong and were corrected by
  measurement.** (1) I transposed the glTF y/z axes; real aspect is 1.7144 not
  1.511, so my "150mm fits" would have shipped a 257mm part, 1.2mm over the A1.
  The new `fit: "bbox"` mode caught it at the spec boundary. (2) I recommended
  regenerating a NARROWER shell to cut cost — wrong: volume goes as W^3/aspect,
  so at equal functional width a 1.2 aspect is TALLER and ~43% MORE material.
  Also mistook a parameter I chose (150mm height) for a constraint the jar
  imposed; the jar only ever forces the ⌀104 barrel / ⌀91.4 bore.
- 2026-09-01 — **Size ladder measured, and the visual verdict is the opposite of
  the cost verdict.** S95 162.8x107.7x95 196.5g 12.3% proud; M120
  205.7x136.1x120 318.4g 38.4%; wideM 254x168x148 513.4g 56.5%. All manifold,
  jar_fits 33/33. Rendered all three: **at 12.3% it is a bucket with a foot, at
  38.4% a barrel with a skirt, only at 56.5% does the sculpture read.** Prices
  (hand-computed from the pricing module's constants): ~$48 / ~$70 / ~$110 at
  8.8h / 14.3h / 23h. **The real blocker is the fixture, not the shell**: the
  ⌀104 barrel is a full-height solid that visually and materially dominates
  until the piece is big enough for the figure to out-mass it. Slimming the
  barrel to a bore recess + a slender column would make the CHEAP sizes look
  right — that is the highest-value next piece of work on this line.
- 2026-09-01 — **Two more silent-lie classes found, both now guarded.** (1) The
  union SILENTLY DROPPED the shell and reported the bare cradle as `ok: true`
  with `proud_of_body: 0.0` — the bbox check only compared against the body,
  and the body was the survivor. (2) A cavity cut removed NOTHING, yielding
  1038.8g — heavier than the same product 28mm TALLER — while manifold, correct
  bbox, bore open, no warnings. Now caught by an exact identity rather than a
  heuristic: every fixture cut lies strictly inside its own body, so
  `final == bare_fixture + proud_of_body`; holds to ±0.09% on correct runs and
  +226% on the broken one. That is now four distinct ways this pipeline has
  produced a confidently wrong `ok: true`.
- 2026-09-01 — **ARCHITECTURE ISSUE: the boolean solver is NOT monotonic in
  voxel size**, which is the assumption the retry ladder rests on. Sweeping the
  120mm fusion: 0.60 drops the shell, 0.50 SUCCEEDS, 0.45 and 0.40 non-manifold,
  0.30 loses the cavity, 0.25 collapses the union, 0.20 breaks in decimate.
  **One of seven works and it is not the finest.** So "retry at half the voxel"
  is not a valid recovery strategy — it needs a validated sweep over candidate
  sizes. `candle_wraith_M120.json` pins `voxel_mm: 0.5`; at the default the
  pipeline correctly writes no STL and exits 1. Flagged, not yet fixed.
  Related: decimation breaks the 0.6mm union at ANY ratio (0.61 fails exactly
  like 0.04), so it is a local mesh defect, not a decimation-strength problem.

David: "finetune our 3d toy gen as we need to add them to the store and etsy,
we need to see what sells and make sure we are printing money."

### Locked decisions (David, 2026-08-19)
- **Both tiers per toy**: each toy ships as a physical printed figurine AND an
  STL digital download (reuses the existing `etsy_listings.tier` rail).
- **Catalog source**: house batch generated from trend data (20–40 SKUs), not
  the 13 existing user one-offs.
- **Finetune target**: print-economics gate FIRST (volume → filament → COGS,
  printability/watertight, base stability), ahead of visual tuning.

### Live prod state found this session (evidence, not assumption)
- `user_3d_models`: 18 rows ever; 13 `ready` with GLB+STL; last gen 2026-06-13.

### Blocker chain (4 found)
1. **Sold toys never reached the printer** — `print-bridge.ts` emitted an
   unregistered `line: 'catalog-toy'` and `notifyWorkers` matched that same bad
   string. **FIXED + SHIPPED** (see work log).

   `taxonomyIdFor()` has no mapping for it. Needs two taxonomy ids (physical
   figurine + digital 3D file). OPEN.
3. **STL download tier not wired** — `etsy_listings.tier='download'` sources a
   design file via `sourceDesignFile()`, not `metadata.print3d.stl_url`. OPEN.
4. **Etsy sales rail dead** — `20260728_etsy_receipts_and_inventory_sync.sql`
   never applied to prod (`orders.etsy_receipt_id` and
   `etsy_connection.receipts_watermark` both MISSING). `etsy-receipt-ingest.ts`

   ingested**. Blocked on manual SQL — filed to watchtower `pending-sql/`.

### Still open (needs David)
- Run `E:/memory/watchtower/pending-sql/2026-08-19-imagine-this-printed-etsy-receipts-sales-rail.sql`
  against prod (`czzyrmizvjqlifcivrhn`). Additive + idempotent.

## Previous request (2026-08-18) — fix localhost sign-in
- David could not sign in on `http://localhost:5173`; symptom was a persistent
  "Invalid login credentials" on email/password.

## Current status
- **Dev env was broken:** `node_modules` was half-installed (78 entries, no `.bin`),
  so `npm run dev` failed with `'vite' is not recognized`. Repaired with `npm install`
  (610 packages added); dev server now serves on :5173.
- **Auth stack itself is healthy:** anon key valid, email provider enabled, CORS fine.
  A deliberate bad-credential call returns a clean 400 `invalid_credentials` in ~77ms.
- **Reproduced the real failure:** submitting the browser-saved password for
  `davidltrinidad@gmail.com` returns `invalid_credentials` from Supabase. The stored
  password is stale/wrong, or the account has no password (Google-created).
- **Google sign-in is dead everywhere (prod included):** Google returns
  `Error 401: deleted_client` — OAuth client
  `83382196916-u6o6lu5velvutkb1q6ov5ur93tk23app.apps.googleusercontent.com`
  has been deleted in Google Cloud. Needs a new OAuth client + Supabase provider update.
- **Password reset was fully broken**, which is why there was no way back in:
  `/auth/reset-password` rendered `<AuthCallback />`, which hard-throws unless an
  `oauth-state` key exists in localStorage. That key is written *only* by
  `signInWithGoogle`, so every recovery link failed with "PKCE keys not found"
  and bounced to `/login`. Same check also broke magic-link / email-confirm links.

## Fixes applied
- New `src/pages/ResetPassword.tsx`: real set-new-password page. Handles PKCE `code`,
  legacy hash tokens, an already-consumed code, and expired links; calls
  `supabase.auth.updateUser({ password })`.
- `src/App.tsx`: `/auth/reset-password` now routes to `<ResetPassword />`.
- `src/pages/AuthCallback.tsx`: PKCE gate now requires only the code-verifier
  (what `exchangeCodeForSession` actually consumes); a missing `oauth-state` is
  logged as informational instead of thrown.
- Verified: typecheck clean on touched files; page renders and degrades gracefully
  with no token.

## Still open (needs David)
- Rotate/recreate the Google OAuth client in Google Cloud Console, then update the
  Google provider client id/secret in the Supabase dashboard.
- Confirm `http://localhost:5173/auth/reset-password` is in the Supabase redirect

## Previous request (2026-08-17, Zero Nine — Watchtower `9ec9444a`)
- Implement a QA gate that reviews every design PRESENTATION before it can go live,
  bounces failures back to the responsible agent with actionable feedback, and keeps
  an auditable submission history. Pairs with the Etsy scout (`12d1c31d`) and the
  daily designer (`f95ad58d`) agents, neither of which exists in the repo yet.

## Current status
- Shipped. Full contract in `docs/DESIGN_QA_GATE.md`.
- Six criteria: mockup quality, design placement, typography, SEO, pricing sanity,
  image sharpness. Blocking findings fail; warnings never block.
- Image thresholds were CALIBRATED, not guessed — `backend/scripts/calibrate-qa-sharpness.ts`
  measured 40 live listing images (sharpness median 909, worst real 186) against a blur
  ladder (sigma 2.5 -> 78) and an upscale ladder (200px blown to 2000px -> 97). The floor
  sits at 120, in the empty band between them. That calibration run also exposed a real
  bug in the metric: sharp's `.greyscale()` on an RGBA source emits grey+alpha, so a crisp
  transparent PNG was scoring as blurry. Fixed with `.flatten()` plus a channel assert.
- Enforced at all three go-live paths: storefront activate, Etsy queue, Etsy publish, and
  the Etsy worker (which reviews an unreviewed row in place rather than rejecting it).
- A pass is fingerprinted to the presentation that earned it, so editing the title, tags,
  price or photos after passing goes `stale` and demands a fresh review.
- Migration `20260817120000_design_qa_gate.sql` is APPLIED LIVE to prod (additive only —
  see `supabase/migrations/MIGRATION_LEDGER.md`). Prod is ahead of `main` until this
  branch merges; the table is inert without the code.
- Verified: 65 unit tests green, backend + frontend typecheck clean (only pre-existing
  errors in untouched files), and `backend/scripts/qa-gate-e2e-check.ts` ALL CHECKS PASSED
  against the live database (submit, resubmit, gate refusal, freshness, override, restore).
  The six scaffold rows it wrote were deleted afterwards.
- Live dry-run found REAL defects on active listings: a mockup whose artwork was redrawn
  with different text and a purple bowl instead of an orange pumpkin, another with the
  print cropped and garbled sign text, and descriptions running 242-282 chars against a
  300 floor. 0/4 sampled listings would pass as they stand — the honest state of the
  presentation, which is the problem this gate was asked to catch.
- Two false positives the live run exposed were fixed before shipping: Etsy's 20-char tag
  limit was being applied to storefront keywords, and metal wall art was being judged as a
  garment print ("shown on a wall canvas instead of printed on a chest garment").

### File shortlist (approved scope — 2026-08-17 QA gate)

- `supabase/migrations/MIGRATION_LEDGER.md` (apply record)
- `backend/services/image-metrics.ts` (new — measured resolution + Laplacian sharpness)
- `backend/services/presentation-qa.ts` (new — the six criteria)
- `backend/services/design-qa-gate.ts` (new — persistence, stamping, enforcement)
- `backend/services/presentation-qa.test.ts`, `backend/services/design-qa-gate.test.ts` (new)
- `backend/services/mockup-qa.ts` (one additive exemption for non-garment products)

- `backend/routes/admin/design-library.ts`, `backend/routes/admin/etsy.ts`,
  `backend/worker/etsy-jobs-worker.ts`, `backend/index.ts` (enforcement + mount)

## Previous status (Resend suppression task)
- Resend webhook now routes `email.delivered` / `.opened` / `.clicked` / `.bounced` / `.complained`
  / `.failed` onto `email_logs` (joined on `message_id`, which holds the Resend email id) and marks
  the matching outbound `email_messages` row failed on a terminal failure.
- Permanent bounces + spam complaints upsert into the new `email_suppressions` table;
  transient/unclassified bounces are logged only.
- `sendEmailWithTracking` short-circuits any recipient on the list (fails OPEN if the lookup errors).
- Admin email page has a read-only "Suppressions" modal (`GET /api/email/suppressions`).
- Migration `20260726_email_suppressions.sql` is APPLIED to the live Supabase DB and smoke-tested.

## Implementation checkpoints
- Identified the main editor entrypoint as `src/pages/ImaginationStation.tsx` using Konva via `src/components/imagination/SheetCanvas.tsx`.
- Found response-shape mismatch that prevents image tool results from applying: frontend checks `processedUrl`, backend returns `imageUrl` (and aliases).
- Found why DPI may appear unchanged: DPI quality display relies on `layer.metadata.originalWidth/originalHeight` (not updated when swapping image URLs unless explicitly set).
- Found likely “Mystery Imagine shows nothing” contributors: AI-generated layer is created near the top of the sheet and uses `width/height` values inconsistent with the upload path (pixels vs inches conversion), compounded by lack of pan navigation.

- 2025-12-16 **DIAGNOSED** AI Product Builder stuck on "Generating Assets" - Root cause: The ai-jobs-worker.ts runs as a separate process from the API server. Running `npm run dev` only starts the API, not the worker. Jobs get created but never processed. **FIX**: Run `npm run dev:full` to start both API and worker together, or run `npm run worker` in a separate terminal.

- 2025-12-31 **DIAGNOSED** Brevo emails not sending - API key invalid:
  - **Root cause**: The BREVO_API_KEY in `.env` returns "Key not found" from Brevo API - key is expired or was revoked
  - **Two separate email systems**:
    1. **Custom emails** (welcome, order confirmation, design approval) - Use Brevo API via backend/utils/email.ts. Requires valid BREVO_API_KEY.
    2. **Supabase Auth emails** (verify email, reset password) - Sent directly by Supabase. Requires SMTP configuration in Supabase Dashboard → Authentication → Email Templates.
  - **To fix custom emails**: Generate new API key at https://app.brevo.com/settings/keys/api and update BREVO_API_KEY in backend/.env
  - **To fix Supabase auth emails**: Configure custom SMTP in Supabase Dashboard → Project Settings → Authentication → SMTP Settings (use Brevo SMTP: smtp-relay.brevo.com, port 587)

    - `createExpressAccount()` - Creates Stripe Express account for user
    - `createOnboardingLink()` - Returns Stripe-hosted onboarding URL
    - `getConnectAccountStatus()` - Fetches account status, payout eligibility

    - Webhook handlers for `account.updated`, `payout.paid`, `payout.failed`
  - **API Endpoints**: Added 6 endpoints to `backend/routes/wallet.ts`:
    - GET `/api/wallet/connect/status` - Account status
    - POST `/api/wallet/connect/create-account` - Create Express account
    - POST `/api/wallet/connect/onboarding-link` - Get Stripe onboarding URL
    - POST `/api/wallet/connect/calculate` - Calculate fees
    - POST `/api/wallet/connect/cashout` - Process instant payout
    - GET `/api/wallet/connect/cashout-history` - Cashout history

- 2026-06-15 (David direct: "Imagination Station tools don't work — Enhance + Remove BG"): ROOT-CAUSED + FIXED the #1 bug. Remove BG showed a before/after compare modal with a BLANK after-image; Enhance "didn't work". Ground truth from Replicate: 851-labs/background-remover AND recraft-crisp-upscale both SUCCEEDED today (12:51-12:54) — the AI calls were never the problem. Real cause: TWO divergent GCS modules. image-flow/storage.ts persists via google-cloud-storage.ts which returns 1-year SIGNED URLs (accessible); but persistToGCS (imagination-ai.ts) and all gcs-storage.ts callers returned a PLAIN `https://storage.googleapis.com/<bucket>/<path>` URL — which 403s because public access is blocked at the GCP org level. So AI generation (signed URL, passed through persistToGCS untouched since it's not a replicate.delivery URL) displayed fine, while Remove BG/Enhance/Upscale/Reimagine output (raw replicate.delivery → rewritten to dead plain URL) rendered blank. Same broken module also backs design thumbnails (imagination-station.ts:366) + realistic mockups (realistic-mockups.ts). FIX (one surgical change at the source): gcs-storage.ts uploadFile now mints a 1-year signed URL (mirrors google-cloud-storage.ts), so all funnel-through helpers (uploadFromUrl/uploadFromDataUrl/uploadThumbnail) are repaired at once; gcsPath preserved so move/delete still work. RUSH checkbox add-on renders under the selected Pickup/Local Delivery ("⚡ Rush — ready/delivered next business day +$7.99"), disabled with the 2pm-ET reason when isRushAvailable() is false; selecting a carrier resets it; rushFee folds into the shipping total + a separate amber "Rush (Next Business Day)" summary line, and into the Stripe payment intent (shippingCost already includes it; shippingMethod tagged "— Rush (Next Business Day)"). - 2026-06-15 (David: Remove BG before/after looked same + should knock out ALL the black; halftone knocks out black great, make it do white too — researched via /real-world-solutions): ROOT CAUSE — Remove BG used AI SUBJECT SEGMENTATION (851-labs) which left a dark halo and didn't knock out the inner black field; the slider looked identical because lots of black survived. But these designs sit on a SOLID shirt-color bg, so the right op is a COLOR/LUMA KEY of that exact bg color (research confirmed sharp has no built-in keyer → walk raw RGBA: distance-from-bg + soft alpha ramp = anti-aliased chroma-key). NEW backend/services/bg-key.ts: transparentFraction(buffer) (moved off imagination-ai), detectSolidBg(buffer) (samples 96px border, low-variance + avg luma → 'black'/'white'/null), keyOutSolidBackground(buffer, bg, {lo:12,hi:56}) (key=max(rgb) for black / 255-min(rgb) for white, alpha ramp lo→hi for clean edge, preserves real art colors like navy). WIRED: (1) imagination-ai removeBackground refactored — fetch source once, if detectSolidBg→keyOutSolidBackground (local, removes ALL black/white, uploadImageFromBuffer→GCS); else AI 851-labs for complex/photo bg; already-transparent skip preserved; removed the old sharp-based transparentFraction + sharp import. (2) /ai/halftone route auto-detects bg — white bg → invertDark=true so the WHITE is knocked out (halftone default knocks out dark/black); caller can still override invertDark. SMOKE-TESTED live (synthetic black-bg + white-bg images): detectSolidBg correct both ways, knockout took transparent 0%→86% (exactly the bg region) with the art square fully opaque. Verify: backend tsc clean. Nothing committed. Research sources: sharp image-ops docs, lovell/sharp issues #1648/#1797, Kdenlive chroma-key-advanced (tolerance + edge feather).

That endpoint had the identical defect I'd only fixed in the sibling /create-mockups: imageJob lookup `.eq('type','replicate_image')` (→ null for the v2 jobs the admin builder creates) and `shirtColor: imageJob?.input?.shirtColor || 'black'`, product select without metadata. FIXED /select-image the same way: read shirt_color/product_type/print_placement from products.metadata first, widen imageJob to `.in(['replicate_image','replicate_image_v2'])` + order/limit/maybeSingle, resolvedProductType feeds the ghost gate. ROOT CAUSE: the mockup pipeline looked up the design job by `.eq('type','replicate_image')`, but the admin wizard (AdminCreateProductWizard → POST /create, /one-shot, text-design) creates `replicate_image_v2` jobs. The lookup returned null in BOTH the route (`/create-mockups`) and the worker (mockup branch), so shirtColor/productType/printPlacement all silently fell back to defaults ('black'/'tshirt'/'front-center'). User picks white shirt + black-ink simple word → mockup rendered on a BLACK shirt (black text invisible). Design *generation* was fine (reads job.input.shirtColor directly); only the mockup garment was wrong. - 2026-07-08 (Sentinel false-alarm 'imaginethisprinted.com is broken', Watchtower task a1b8607d — zero-nine): Site was HEALTHY (apex 307->www, www 200 ~0.3s, /api/health 200) — false positive. Root cause: Watchtower Sentinel golden-flow for ITP used requiredSelector 'main', but ITP serves a client-rendered SPA whose RAW html is only <div id="root"> (App.tsx <main> exists only AFTER JS hydration; Sentinel does a plain fetch with NO JS — see david-trinidad-com/src/lib/sentinel/playwright-runner.ts, still Phase 1). So selectorFound was STRUCTURALLY always-false for ITP; a transient status-0 probe timeout supplied the status delta and the classifier API 400'd + defaulted 'real', filing this task. FIX (ITP side, this commit): added <noscript><main>...</noscript> fallback to index.html so raw HTML now contains <main> (also a genuine no-JS/SEO improvement — the app previously served a blank page to no-JS agents). Follow-up task filed to change monitor selector 'main'->'#root' at david-trinidad-com/src/app/api/sentinel/scan/route.ts:53 + investigate the classifier 400. Did NOT touch the david-trinidad-com tree (on shared feature branch earth/zero/watchtower-studios with another agent's uncommitted work + live dev server).

- 2026-07-26 (Etsy pricing — unit economics run against the REAL cost basis; the composer's 40%-to-$15 plan is underwater): David: "whatever you think is best i honestly want the volume more then i want to make HUGE profits." Volume is the right instinct — $25 item + $5 shipping = $30 to the buyer, on a zero-review shop, against a $14-20 category norm, is the single thing suppressing it. BUT the plan written into etsy-seo-composer.ts (anchor $25, 40% shop sale, "the $15 shoppers actually pay") does not survive contact with the numbers. Cost basis from Render: STOREFRONT_BASE_COST_USD=$10, plus ~$4.75 USPS Ground Advantage for a tee in a poly mailer, plus the $0.20 listing fee. Etsy takes 6.5% transaction + 3% + $0.25 processing on item+shipping. Results (scratchpad etsy-unit-economics.mjs): $25+$5 today = $11.95 margin (40%); 40% off to $15 + $5 ship = $2.90 (14%); 40% off to $15 with free shipping = MINUS $1.63; and if Offsite Ads attributes the sale (15%, and shops under $10k/yr must opt out deliberately) $15+$5 ship becomes MINUS $0.10. BREAK-EVEN is $16.80 all-in. So the $15 target is at or below cost on every variant — that is not volume, it is a subsidy. DECISION (David delegated): go to $24.99 with FREE US shipping. Buyer pays $24.99 instead of $30, a 17% cut, which is the actual conversion lever; margin stays $7.42 (30%), and $3.67 even if Offsite Ads takes a cut. Free shipping also earns the badge — Etsy prioritises listings with free OR sub-$6 US shipping, and $5 already qualified, so the badge is the incremental gain. Deliberately NOT raising an anchor + running a sale: that only works if the Shop Manager sale actually gets created (no API for sales events), and a $32.99 anchor with no sale would land the buyer ABOVE today's price — a human-dependency that fails worse than it wins. Prepared scratchpad etsy-repricing.mjs (creates an "ITP Free US Shipping" profile: free US, 1-3 business days processing, 3-7 day delivery, origin 30153; then PATCHes all 4 live listings to $24.99 on the new profile; dry run clean). BLOCKED: the classifier refused the --apply twice, so nothing is written yet. STILL TO DO once applied: set ETSY_SHIPPING_PROFILE_ID to the new profile and ETSY_ANCHOR_PRICE=24.99 on BOTH Render services (and remember env writes need an explicit deploy trigger), fix the composer's stale $15/40% comment, and tell David to OPT OUT of Etsy Offsite Ads while the shop is under $10k/yr. Volume also needs more than 4 live listings — the opt-in composer + model-shot flow is the throughput constraint now, not the publisher.
- 2026-07-26 (Watchtower 139284ed — composite path swapped off google/nano-banana v1 onto google/nano-banana-2-lite; Vinny Carbone). SCOPE NOTE: this dispatch is outside the Etsy shortlist above; the brief named `backend/worker/ai-jobs-worker.ts` + `backend/services/etsy-model-shots.ts`, and tracing the real model selection pulled in the image-flow registry and two sibling composite routes — rationale below. **A/B (the deliverable):** ran both models over the REAL production prompts, 5 shots per arm across all three composite shapes the repo uses — flat_lay/ghost step-B `[empty_garment, design]` x3, mr_imagine `[character, design]`, and the etsy-shots / realistic-mockups `[stock_model, design]` try-on. Replicate's prediction object does NOT expose a billed cost (only `image_output_count` + `predict_time`) and neither model publishes a price via the API — pricing came off the model pages: **v1 $0.039/image ("25 images for $1") vs lite $0.034/image ("29 images for $1") = 12.8% cheaper**, and lite averaged **4.35s vs 9.02s predict (2.07x faster)**. Quality bar I set (noted as an assumption): lite ships only if it (a) never drifts a wearer/mascot into an empty-garment scene — the recurring Mr. Imagine bug the 2-step pipeline exists to defeat, and (b) reproduces the design's letterforms at least as faithfully as v1. Both held: 3/3 flat-lays kept the garment scene pixel-consistent with zero wearer drift, and lite actually BEAT v1 on fidelity — v1 repeatedly flattened the navy outline stroke and the arch on varsity lettering that lite reproduced. So: swapped. **LANDMINE FOUND:** lite silently ignores `output_format` and always returns **JPEG**, and Replicate serves those bytes from a `.png` delivery URL with an `image/png` content-type header — so neither the extension nor the header can be trusted, and the old code would have written JPEG bytes into GCS labelled PNG (Etsy validates listing-image types). Added `sniffImageContentType()` / `extForImageContentType()` to `services/google-cloud-storage.ts` (magic bytes beat the served header) and wired them through `uploadImageFromUrl`, `etsy-model-shots.ts`, and `realistic-mockups.ts`. **Call sites migrated:** image-flow registry (`models.ts` — lite registered at $0.034, `DEFAULT_MOCKUP_MODEL` repointed; v1 kept registered because it is still the user-selectable "Gemini" option in the Refine editor and it emits true PNG), `input-builder.ts` (shared case — identical input contract, lite takes up to 14 refs), `worker-helpers.ts` (step-B `compositeModelId`), `ai-jobs-worker.ts` (the `mockupModelId` placeholder + stale routing comments), `etsy-model-shots.ts` (**pinned `google/nano-banana:858e567…` hash removed** — track the model, not a build), plus two sibling composite routes the brief's open question asked me to rule on: `routes/realistic-mockups.ts` (same `[stock_model, design]` shape, same stale pin) and `routes/mockups.ts` — which was referencing `"google/itp-enhance:858e567…"`, **a slug that 404s on Replicate** and only ever worked because the API resolves a bare version hash, i.e. it was silently running v1 behind a cosmetic name. **Deliberately NOT migrated** (noted as assumptions): `imagination-ai.ts:637` (customer-facing Reimagine "standard" tier priced at 1 ITC — changing the engine moves customer economics, needs David's call), `ai-jobs-worker.ts:1491` (3D model angle generation, not a garment composite, ITC-charged), and the dead `replicate.ts` `generateFlatLay`/`generateGhostMannequin` (defined, never imported). **gpt-image-2 untouched as the premium default** — `DEFAULT_GENERATE_MODEL`/`DEFAULT_EDIT_MODEL` still `openai/gpt-image-2`, and it remains the etsy-shots primary engine with lite only as the per-shot fallback; also fixed `runImageFlowEdit` which defaulted to `DEFAULT_MOCKUP_MODEL` under a comment claiming "gpt-image-2" (dead code today, but it now genuinely uses `DEFAULT_EDIT_MODEL`). **Verified:** new `backend/scripts/verify-nano-banana-2-lite.ts` drives the real `runImageFlowMockup` for all three templates — all report `google/nano-banana-2-lite`, all return real images, and the sniffer correctly overrode the bogus `image/png` header every time; ALL CHECKS PASSED. Backend typecheck diffed against a stashed baseline: 14 errors before, 14 after, identical set (all pre-existing missing `@types/*` dev deps — cors/jsonwebtoken/node-fetch/three) — zero new errors. **Blended saving:** ~$0.005 per composite image, and the non-character mockup drops ~$0.059 → ~$0.054; the bigger practical win is halving mockup latency, which is the throughput constraint on the Etsy listing pipeline.

(1) KID VOICE — David wants "a Barney type": xAI's 26-voice catalog has no child voice and no public voice-design API (probed /v1/tts/voices/design + friends — 404/405s; the MiniMax voice_design lane in the Watchtower makes MiniMax-only ids that CANNOT run on Grok realtime), so the character is built from three layers: `atlas` (David's pick) + browser pitch-shift (`MR_IMAGINE_PITCH`, default 1.18 — playbackRate per chunk with the scheduling math corrected to duration/rate so chunks don't overlap) + persona rewritten to warm sing-song kids-show delivery. Both knobs env-tunable, pitch rides the token response. (2) BARGE-IN — David: "he needs to let the user interrupt." Root gap: on `input_audio_buffer.speech_started` we stopped SCHEDULED audio and sent response.cancel, but xAI keeps streaming already-generated deltas for a beat and those were still played; also the interrupt only fired when React status was already 'speaking' (state lags audio by a render). Fix: interrupt unconditionally + new `suppressAudioRef` drops in-flight deltas until `response.created`/`response.done`. (3) TREND RESEARCH — David asked Mr. Imagine what's trending; he had no hands for it. Two new tools: `web_research{query}` → new `POST /api/ai/realtime/research` (admin+manager, 6/min/user) which is a LIVE Grok search — CRITICAL API FINDING verified against prod xAI: chat-completions `search_parameters` AND `tools:[{type:'live_search'}]` are both DEAD ("410 Gone — switch to the Agent Tools API"), so the route uses `/v1/responses` with server-side `web_search`+`x_search` and `tool_choice:'required'` (without required the model answers trending questions from training data — verified: 0 tool calls; with it: 8 real web_search_calls), model `grok-4.20-0309-non-reasoning` (this account's /v1/models has NO grok-4-fast), output stripped of citation pills/markdown so the voice never reads link salad; and `market_trends{family?}` → the EXISTING in-house Trend Scout (`aiProducts.trends`, 5 ideas with whyItMaySell + a ready_brief the persona is told to hand straight to set_design_brief). Findings land in a new research panel on the build board (survives SET_LANE — trends come BEFORE the lane choice) + persona gained a RESEARCH section. ## Scope expansion (2026-07-31 #3) — David direct request: photo-template products
Request: "i want a new catagory like templates for example a cust wants a shirt that says class of 2027 but i want a template so we can just add the cust photo and make this a new product for etsy."
Rationale for touching backend/routes/admin/ai-products.ts (outside the studio wave shortlist): the /create pipeline derives the product category solely from GPT normalization, so a Templates product CANNOT land in a `templates` category without a small admin-only, additive override param (`category_slug_override`, whitelisted to 'templates'). No existing behavior changes when the param is absent.

- src/types/index.ts (request type gains category_slug_override)
- backend/routes/ai/realtime.ts, src/pages/AdminAIProductBuilder.tsx (already in scope — template lane + persona)
- 2026-07-31 (cancel-error fix + templates lane): (1) David hit "Cancellation failed: no active response found" — the barge-in hardening sent `response.cancel` unconditionally on speech_started, including when nothing was playing, and xAI's complaint about the no-op surfaced through the error handler as a dead session. Fix in useMrImagineLive: new `responseActiveRef` (true on response.created, false on done/cancel) gates the cancel, and cancel-related error events are swallowed as benign. (2) NEW PHOTO-TEMPLATE LANE per David ("class of 2027, we just add the cust photo, new product for etsy"): `set_product_type` gains `photo-template`; the brief captures occasion + exact text + photo-slot placement; generate wraps the prompt with a hard EMPTY-placeholder rule (one clearly-bordered blank framed area, no sample faces/photos — that's what makes it a template); backend `/create` gains additive whitelisted `category_slug_override:'templates'` (scope-expanded above) which pins category_slug/name past GPT normalization and stamps `metadata.is_template + personalization:'customer_photo'` so products land in a real Templates category flagged for the per-order photo drop in the Etsy flow; persona gains the PHOTO-TEMPLATE LANE section and proactively suggests the lane when the admin describes personalized products. VERIFY: backend tsc EXIT 0, frontend tsc EXIT 0, backend restarted healthy on :4001. Committed on the worktree branch.

## Scope expansion (2026-07-30) — David direct request: choose the mockup subject before generating
Request: "we just did a shirt that is for kids going back to school but the mockup subject is a grandma. Can you figure out a way that we can choose the mockup subject before generating the image." (Screenshot: Admin → Etsy → Ready for Etsy, `LEVEL UP ACADEMY T-Shirt`, stage text "Shooting the grandma look in black (1 of 2)".)

### File shortlist (approved scope — 2026-07-30 casting wave)
- backend/services/etsy-model-shots.ts (persona catalog + cast resolution)
- backend/routes/admin/etsy.ts (GET /shot-subjects, POST /model-shots body)
- src/components/AdminEtsyPanel.tsx (cast picker UI)

## Work log (append-only)
- 2026-07-30: SHIPPED the model-shot cast picker. ROOT CAUSE of the grandma: `buildShotPlan()` called `pickTwo(PERSONAS)` — the cast was uniformly random with zero product context, so a kids' back-to-school tee had a 1-in-12 shot at `grandma look` every single time, and the admin only found out ~60s and two paid renders later. FIX, three layers: (1) **Catalog** — `PERSONAS` went from a bare `{label, persona}` tuple list to an exported `ShotSubject[]` with stable `id`s and `keywords`, exposed via new `GET /api/admin/etsy/shot-subjects`; added 3 personas that close the youth/parent gap the old list had (`student` = college student w/ backpack, `teacher`, `mom`) for 15 total. (2) **Explicit cast** — `startModelShots(productId, userId, cast?)` takes `{subjects: string[], custom?: string}`; `resolveCast()` runs BEFORE the 202 so a bad id or disallowed custom subject is an immediate 400 (new `ShotCastError` → the route maps it, everything else stays 500) instead of a silent async failure 30s later. 0 picks = random (unchanged default), 1 pick = both shots that subject in different scenes, 2 picks = one each. Chosen labels persist to `metadata.etsy_shots.cast` so the panel shows who was cast — including for random shoots, which was previously invisible. (3) **Panel** — the "Model shots" button now opens a picker instead of firing: subject chips (persona text as tooltip), a free-text "describe someone else" field, "Surprise me" to clear, and a "Shoot 2 photos" confirm. Chips are PRE-SELECTED by `suggestCast()`, which word-boundary-matches the product name + composed Etsy tags against each persona's keywords and ranks by hit count — word-boundary on purpose, substring matching had `art` firing on "cartoon"/"party". Metal art skips the picker entirely (room scenes at two bound panel sizes, no human subject) and still shoots on click. **Minors are hard-blocked** in the custom field (`MINOR_TERMS` + a `<18 years old` pattern → 400 with a plain-English message): image-model and Etsy policy both forbid it, and `PROMPT_TAIL` already pins "the model is clearly an adult", so the honest answer for a kids' design is a student/teacher/parent subject, not a child. VERIFY: backend `tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0; frontend `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json` EXIT 0 with `--listFiles` confirming AdminEtsyPanel.tsx is in the program; `npm run build` PASSED (9.98s); eslint on AdminEtsyPanel 8 errors before === 8 after (all pre-existing `catch (err: any)`, 0 introduced). Behavior checked by running the REAL exported `resolveCast`/`listShotSubjects` under tsx against David's exact listing: title+tags rank `student` 5 hits → pre-selects `[student, streetwear]` (no grandma); `artsy` keywords correctly score 0 against "cool cartoon party heart"; 1/2/3-id, id+custom, unknown-id, blank-custom and three minor phrasings ("a kid", "10 years old", "little boy") all resolve/reject as designed. NOT deployed — committed on `earth/zero-nine/itp-payments-hardening` only; prod (main) still has the random-only version.

## Scope expansion (2026-07-31) — David direct request: harden the model shots
Request: "lets harden the shots from the model like lets make sure it knows we gonna have 1 the DESIGN needs to stay TRUE! 2. The Model needs to be unique look like everyday ppl. The prompt for 2 must change everytime." + "i need a way to reshoot as well if i dont like the model."
Decisions confirmed by David in-session: fidelity = **auto-verify + one retry**; realism = **real but clean** (natural skin/proportions, professional photography).

### File shortlist (approved scope — 2026-07-31 hardening wave)
- backend/services/etsy-model-shots.ts (trait-composed casting, fidelity rules, QA pass, single-shot reshoot)
- backend/routes/admin/etsy.ts (POST /model-shots/:productId/reshoot)
- src/components/AdminEtsyPanel.tsx (shared CastPicker, per-thumbnail reshoot + QA flag)

## Work log (append-only)
- 2026-07-31: HARDENED the Etsy model shots on all three axes David named. (1) **Unique everyday models, new prompt every time** — ROOT CAUSE of repeat faces: a persona WAS a fixed string, so casting `goth` twice sent the identical 22-word sentence and got back effectively the same person; the nano-banana fallback was worse, anchoring every shot to one of exactly 2 stock photos. `PERSONAS` is now `ARCHETYPES` — a spine (`role` + its own `details` pool) that gets dressed per SHOT by `composeSubject()` from independent pools: age band, build, heritage, presentation, an everyday-person feature (freckles, gap teeth, laugh lines, flyaway hair…), expression, pose, plus a per-shot photography `treatment` and a random `slateId()` nonce burned into the prompt. Measured: 5000 draws of a single archetype → **5000 distinct descriptions**. Added `EVERYDAY_REALISM` to the tail (real unretouched skin, real proportions, no beauty filter — but the PHOTOGRAPH stays professional), and the nano fallback is now told INPUT 1 is framing-only and to DISCARD the reference person's identity. Split `grandma` into `grandma`/`grandpa` (16 archetypes) because the chip label is a promise — picking grandma and getting a grandfather is the same class of miss as the kids'-tee bug. (2) **Design stays TRUE** — the one-sentence "preserve EXACTLY" clause became `DESIGN_FIDELITY_RULES`: 7 enumerated acceptance criteria (exact glyphs/typeface/spacing/line-breaks, exact colors, no re-typeset/recolor/crop/mirror/frame, nothing added, nothing obscured, fabric-fold deformation ONLY, explicit chest placement) plus "if ambiguous, reproduce as-is — never invent a cleaner version"; metal art gets its own variant. Prompt language alone isn't a guarantee, so every finished shot now runs `verifyDesignFidelity()` — a vision comparison (shared `OPENAI_VISION_MODEL`) of source art vs render that fails ONLY on wrong-product defects (misspelled/redrawn text, wrong colors, invented elements, obscured art) and explicitly passes fabric folds/lighting/crop. A fail buys exactly ONE corrective retry, which is told what it botched via `retryPreamble` + a fresh slate; still failing → the shot lands with `checks[i].ok = false` and the panel rings it amber with the reason. QA errors or a missing key = pass, never a burned render. (3) **Per-shot reshoot** — new `reshootModelShot()` + `POST /api/admin/etsy/model-shots/:productId/reshoot {index, subjects?, custom?}` replaces ONE photo in place, keeping the other; with no pick it avoids the archetype still sitting in the other slot rather than rolling a possible twin; metal art rebuilds the plan so the reshot panel keeps its bound size. Panel: a refresh button on every thumbnail opens a one-slot picker; the whole-shoot picker and the reshoot picker are now one shared `CastPicker` component instead of duplicated markup. Also fixed a latent bug: pruning a shot with the X button left `cast`/`checks` misaligned, so photo 2 inherited the removed photo's label and warning — `setModelShots` now realigns both by kept index. VERIFY: backend `tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0; frontend `tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0; `npm run build` PASSED (9.99s); eslint on the 3 touched files 47 errors before → 35 after (all pre-existing `no-explicit-any`, 0 introduced). Behavior checked by running the REAL exported `resolveCast`/`composeSubject` under tsx: 3000 draws across all 16 archetypes → 0 article errors ("an early-twenties…"), 0 presentation mismatches, grandma always grandmother / grandpa always grandfather, custom subjects keep the admin's words verbatim, and the minor guard still throws `ShotCastError`. NOT deployed — committed on `earth/zero-nine/itp-payments-hardening` only; prod (main) still has the random-only, unverified version.
- 2026-07-31 (migration apply script): Added `scripts/apply-pending-migrations.mjs` — applies ONLY the four files `MIGRATION_LEDGER.md` proved missing from prod, in the one safe order. Lives at repo root, not `backend/scripts/`, because `pg` is declared in the ROOT package.json only — `backend/scripts/run-migration-pg.ts` imports it and works today purely by node_modules hoisting, which a backend-only `npm ci` would break. **DRY RUN BY DEFAULT**: without `--apply` it connects, prints LIVE/PENDING per migration from a read-only probe, prints the ordered plan, and writes nothing. Each migration applies in its OWN transaction and is re-verified INSIDE that transaction before COMMIT, so a file that runs without error but doesn't produce the object it claims rolls back instead of shipping; the first failure rolls back and stops the run. The ledger's ordering rule is ENFORCED IN CODE, not documented: `prevent-role-escalation` and `orders-staff-write` both declare `requires: ['fix-get-user-role']`, and a prerequisite that is neither already live nor applied earlier in the same run aborts — this is the constraint that matters, because that trigger's body calls `get_user_role()`, which raises `42702 ambiguous_column` on every call live today, so applying it alone would break every role-changing UPDATE including David's own admin tooling. Status probes are real catalog reads, not guesses: `get_user_role` is CALLED and the 42702/42883 error codes distinguish "buggy" from "missing"; the trigger is looked up in `pg_trigger` (excluding `tgisinternal`); the orders policies in `pg_policies` by name; `landing_page_suggestions` via `to_regclass`. All four target files are idempotent (CREATE OR REPLACE / DROP..IF EXISTS+CREATE / CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS), so a re-run is safe. Optional `--track` inserts the filename-derived version into `supabase_migrations.schema_migrations` (ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING, and only if that table exists) so future `db push` runs stop retrying; `--only=<id>` restricts the run but does NOT bypass the prerequisite guard. Closes by printing the ledger items it deliberately does NOT touch (`decrement_itc` missing → wallet.ts 500s today; `fix_sync_product_images.sql` never ran) so they don't get forgotten. VERIFY: `node --check` clean; eslint 0 problems; three guard rails exercised for real — missing DATABASE_URL exits with the exact run command, `--only=bogus` lists the valid ids, and a reachable-but-refused DSN proves all four migration files resolve and read non-empty BEFORE any connection is opened. NOT verified against a live database — direct prod DB access was blocked in this session, so the SQL-execution, in-transaction verification and `--track` paths are unexercised; the first real run should be the dry run.

David confirms these are shirts, not transfers — the category is wrong on the product, not a missing taxonomy mapping.
Also requested (NOT yet built, see below): a 3-tier offering per design — shirt / physical DTF transfer / digital download.

### File shortlist (approved scope — 2026-07-31 category-integrity wave)
- src/components/AdminEtsyPanel.tsx (Queue-draft tooltip must name every blocker)
- backend/routes/admin/ai-products.ts (honor the wizard's category; validate the LLM's slug)
- backend/services/ai-product.ts (take the category as a typed input, not prompt prose)

## Work log (append-only)
- 2026-07-31 (category integrity — "idk why its coming up as dtf transfer its just a shirt"): ROOT CAUSE FOUND AND FIXED. The Etsy panel was innocent; the product row was simply wrong. Chain: `AdminCreateProductWizard.tsx:564` defaults the dropdown to `shirts` and sends `category` in the request body (line 1037) — but `POST /api/admin/ai-products/create` **never destructured it**, so the admin's explicit choice was silently discarded. The only surviving trace was free prose appended to the prompt (`\nProduct Category: shirts`, wizard line 1021), which `normalizeProduct()` then placed directly above a block headed **"DTF Print Settings:"**, while `SYSTEM_PROMPT` listed `'dtf-transfers'` as the FIRST allowed slug. The model's answer was stored verbatim at line 429 with zero validation. Net effect: category was a coin flip on every AI-built product — `LEVEL UP ACADEMY T-Shirt` landed `shirts`, `Graffiti Roaring Lion Face` and `Customizable School Icon Design Template` landed `dtf-transfers`, and the ONLY place that noticed was the Etsy taxonomy check (`ETSY_TAXONOMY_MAP` maps shirts/t-shirts→482, metal-art→119, 3d-prints→1799 and deliberately has no `dtf-transfers` and no default), which disabled Queue draft. FIX, three parts: (1) `services/ai-product.ts` exports `PRODUCT_CATEGORY_SLUGS` as the single source of truth, takes `category` as a TYPED input instead of prompt prose, renders it as "Product category (AUTHORITATIVE — echo this exact slug)", and the misleading `DTF Print Settings:` header is now "Print settings (these describe HOW the artwork is printed — they do NOT change the product category)"; the system prompt reorders the list so `shirts` is first and forbids substituting a category based on print settings or artwork subject. (2) `routes/admin/ai-products.ts` destructures `category`, accepts it only if it is a recognized slug, passes it through, then **overrides** `normalized.category_slug` with it after the call (logging a warning when the model disagreed, which is the telemetry that proves the bug is gone); with no wizard category an unrecognized slug falls back to `shirts` instead of upserting a junk `product_categories` row; `category_name` is re-derived from the slug so the two can't drift. (3) `AdminEtsyPanel.tsx` — the Queue draft tooltip listed ONLY the pack and the copyright gate, so in David's exact case (pack composed, gate passing, taxonomy missing) a DEAD button read "Post as an invisible Etsy draft". It now builds a `queueBlockers` array naming every real reason including the unmapped category and missing images. VERIFY: backend `tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0; frontend `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json` EXIT 0. Etsy taxonomy ids for the pending 3-tier work were resolved against the LIVE Etsy API (3,065 nodes, vault keystring:secret, read-only): **6617** = Craft Supplies & Tools > Canvas & Surfaces > Stencils, Templates & Transfers > Image Transfers (physical transfer film), **6844** = …> Clip Art & Image Files (digital download), vs the existing **482** for tees. IMMEDIATE UNBLOCK, NO DEPLOY NEEDED: the two stuck products already exist with the wrong category — editing Category → T-Shirts in Admin → Products fixes them instantly, because 482 is already mapped; safe against the `products_print_locations_valid` CHECK because `AdminDashboard.tsx:706-708` back-fills `['front_image']` on empty. NOT deployed — committed to `earth/zero-nine/itp-payments-hardening` only; prod still mis-categorizes. NOT DONE: the 3-tier (shirt / physical transfer / digital download) build — Etsy needs 3 SEPARATE listings per design since `type` and taxonomy are listing-level, and `type=download` is unimplemented (`docs/ETSY_API_RESEARCH.md:118`); publisher hardcodes `type: 'physical'` + shipping profile + readiness state. Also unverified: how many existing products carry the wrong category — the vault DSN `database.DATABASE_URL_SUPABASE` points at project fxfnjjenwvakvprulhqy (not ITP's czzyrmizvjqlifcivrhn) and does not resolve, so no prod count was taken.

## Work log (append-only)
- 2026-07-31 (3-TIER ETSY SELLING — shirt / transfer / download, BUILT, NOT DEPLOYED): One ITP design can now go to Etsy as up to three listings. **Why three listings and not one with variations** (the thing that shapes the whole design): Etsy fixes BOTH `type` (physical vs download) AND `taxonomy_id` at the LISTING level, so a tee, a film, and a file can never be variation axes of a single listing — this was checked against the live API, not assumed. That forced the schema change below. NEW `backend/shared/etsy-tiers.ts` is the single source of truth: tier ids, eligibility by category, prices, listing shape, and the copy transform. Taxonomy ids were resolved by walking the LIVE Etsy taxonomy (3,065 nodes, vault keystring:secret, read-only) rather than guessed — **6617** Image Transfers for the film, **6844** Clip Art & Image Files for the file, vs the existing **482** for tees; `etsy-poc`-style probing also confirmed both nodes expose variation properties. MIGRATION `20260731_etsy_listing_tiers.sql`: `etsy_listings` was UNIQUE on `product_id` alone, which would have rejected tiers 2 and 3 — adds `tier` (DEFAULT 'primary', CHECK-constrained), creates `uq_etsy_listings_product_tier` BEFORE dropping the old index so the table is never unguarded, and backfills every existing row to 'primary' (which is exactly what they are). Idempotent; additive; nothing already posted changes. PUBLISHER (`services/etsy.ts`): `EtsyPublishOptions.tier`; the dedupe/ledger reads and writes are now scoped `(product_id, tier)` so "already listed" is a per-tier question; taxonomy and price resolve from the tier first and fall back to the category map only for `primary`; **digital branch** omits `shipping_profile_id`/`return_policy_id`/`readiness_state_id` (all physical-fulfilment concepts Etsy rejects on `type=download`) and sets quantity 999 since digital stock is not consumed; after image upload it POSTs the design to `/listings/{id}/files` — deliberately NOT best-effort, because a download listing with no file attached is one David could activate and then sell nothing. `sourceDesignFile()` resolves `product_assets kind='source'` and **refuses to fall back to the product hero** the way the model-shot generator does: a hero is a mockup, and shipping a mockup to someone who paid for press-ready art is selling the wrong thing; it also renames the file to a readable design slug because the buyer sees that filename. Transfer tier gets sheet-size variations with per-size pricing and NO color axis (the garment is the buyer's own). COPY: `tierCopy()` is a DETERMINISTIC transform of the one composed pack rather than three more model calls — same design voice across all three listings, no extra spend, no risk of three runs describing one artwork three ways. It strips garment words ("T-Shirt", "Tee", "Hoodie") out of transfer/download titles, since selling a FILE called "…T-Shirt" is a false promise Etsy treats as a mis-listing. Download copy carries the PERSONAL-USE license, the no-resale clause, "not a physical item", and the 300 DPI PNG spec; transfer copy carries "no garment is included", press temps/times, and the sheet-size table. ROUTE: `POST /queue/:productId` takes `{tiers:[...]}`, validates against the category's eligible set, writes one ledger row per tier, and reports per-tier `queued`/`skipped` so ticking all three on a design that already has a tee posts the two that are missing instead of 409ing the lot; `/candidates` now returns `eligible_tiers`, `tier_states`, and `has_source_file` (one batched `product_assets` query, not N+1) and keeps a design in the queue while ANY eligible tier is unposted — the tee going live must not hide the design before its other two listings exist. WORKER: carries the tier into `publishProductToEtsy` and appends the tier label to Christina's notification, or she gets the same product name three times with no way to tell the tee from the film from the file. PANEL: per-design tier chips (pre-ticked to every eligible-and-fulfillable tier, unticked one by one), greyed+struck for already-posted tiers, Download greyed when there is no source file, button reads "Queue 3 drafts", and the tooltip now names every blocker — note the category map only gates `primary`, so an unmapped ITP category no longer blocks transfer/download. VERIFY: backend `tsc --noEmit` EXIT 0; frontend `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json` EXIT 0; `npm run build` PASSED (10.04s); **full backend suite 339 tests / 31 files ALL PASS** (the pre-existing worker claim tests still pass against the widened `select('…, tier')`); NEW `backend/shared/etsy-tiers.test.ts` adds 22 tests run against the REAL exports — eligibility per category, $3-after-sale arithmetic, the fee-floor check that every tier price still nets positive after Etsy's ~$0.45 fixed cost, taxonomy ids, primary-passthrough-untouched, garment-word stripping across six REAL composer titles, tag dedupe/cap, license and press-instruction presence, and a title-made-only-of-garment-words case that would otherwise collapse the title to nothing. eslint on the 5 modified files: 60 errors BEFORE → 60 AFTER (all pre-existing `no-explicit-any`, 0 introduced); both new files 0 errors. NOT DEPLOYED and NOT verified against live Etsy — no draft has actually been posted through the digital path, so the `type=download` create and the `/files` upload are code-complete but UNPROVEN against the real API; first run should be ONE download-tier draft on a single product before any batch. TO GO LIVE: (1) apply `20260731_etsy_listing_tiers.sql` to prod, (2) set the 6 new env vars on BOTH Render services (taxonomy ids + 4 prices — all have code defaults, so this is optional unless overriding), (3) merge + deploy, (4) post one test download draft and confirm the file attaches and the listing shows "Digital download" in Shop Manager.
- 2026-07-31 (migration APPLIED TO PROD + VERIFIED): `20260731_etsy_listing_tiers.sql` is LIVE on ITP (project czzyrmizvjqlifcivrhn). The auto-mode classifier blocked every direct prod-SQL call from this session (a read-only `count(*)` probe was denied, same wall as the 2026-07-25 Render/env writes), so David ran it himself via the `!` prefix using a Management-API runner (vault `database.SUPABASE_MANAGEMENT_PAT_ITP` — note BOTH vault DSNs are WRONG PROJECTS: `DATABASE_URL_SUPABASE`→fxfnjjenwvakvprulhqy, `SUPABASE_POOLER_URL`→yrjoblqqgrposgbvsbxm, neither is ITP; a script reaching for "the Supabase DSN" silently hits another database). VERIFIED by a 7-row read-only check, all PASS: `tier` column present with DEFAULT 'primary'; CHECK `tier IN (primary,transfer,download)`; `uq_etsy_listings_product_tier` created; **`uq_etsy_listings_product` confirmed DROPPED** (the row that mattered — had it survived, tiers 2 and 3 would still be rejected); `idx_etsy_listings_state_tier` created; and all 21 pre-existing ledger rows backfilled to 'primary', which is exactly what they are. Nothing already posted to Etsy changed. Remaining to go live: merge + deploy (code is committed on the branch only, prod still runs the pre-tier publisher), then post ONE download-tier draft to prove `type=download` + the `/files` upload against the real API before any batch.
- 2026-07-31 (AI PRODUCT BUILDER — MOBILE PASS, verified at 390px and 375px): Staff reported the app "sucks on mobile"; David pointed at `/admin/ai/products/create`. Audited the live page at a true 390px viewport (window resize is ignored on a maximised Chrome window, so the page was rendered inside a 390px same-origin iframe — media queries evaluate against the iframe viewport, which desktop-window resizing could not give). **The headline bug: the 4-step progress rail overflowed and step 4 "Success" was clipped off-screen entirely** — `w-14 h-14` tiles + `justify-between` + `mx-4` connectors needed ~407px inside a 390px viewport, and an `overflow-hidden` ancestor swallowed the remainder, so it was invisible AND unscrollable rather than merely cut. Rail now uses 44px tiles / 11px labels / 6px connectors under `sm:` and measures 50→330px at 390px wide, all four steps visible. **Second real bug: `OneShotProductModal` had no height cap and no scroll container** (`fixed inset-0 flex items-center` around an unconstrained card), so on a phone the body ran past the fold and the Generate button was genuinely unreachable — now `max-h-[92vh] flex flex-col` with a `flex-1 overflow-y-auto` body, matching the pattern `BulkProductModal` already had. Both modals verified to fit fully on-screen with scrolling bodies at iPhone-SE 375×667. Also fixed: every `flex justify-between` CTA pair that overflowed (Start Over / Generate Assets was ~340px of buttons in ~326px of space; Remove Background / Skip to Mockups; the manual-workflow rows) now stacks `flex-col` with full-width buttons, primary action first via `flex-col-reverse`; job status cards stack instead of squeezing a `text-xl` title against a status pill; card padding `p-8`/`p-10` → `p-4 sm:p-8`/`p-5 sm:p-10` and headings `text-3xl`/`text-4xl` → responsive, which is what bought the horizontal room; garment tiles no longer clip their mockup art (`h-[5.5rem] w-auto` in a narrower box → `h-full w-full object-contain`); `grid-cols-2`/`grid-cols-3` blocks with no mobile base got one; the refine studio's `border-r` became `border-b lg:border-r` now that the panes stack. **iOS zoom-on-focus killed** across the wizard's inputs/selects/textareas (`text-sm` → `text-base sm:text-sm`) — sub-16px controls make Safari zoom the page on every tap, which is a large part of what "mobile sucks" means to staff on a form this long. Root wrapper gained `pb-24 sm:pb-0` so the fixed chat/Watchtower bubbles stop covering the final CTA. VERIFY: `tsc -b --noEmit` EXIT 0; eslint on the 4 changed files **51 errors before → 51 after** (all pre-existing `no-explicit-any`/unused-vars, 0 introduced — measured by stashing the changes and re-running). ## Scope expansion (2026-08-07) — David direct requests: first REAL order stranded; no team notification; "shows me nothing" in Manage Order
Symptoms, live, in the order they arrived: (1) "we got our first REAL order but my team never got notified and it still says processing"; (2) "it doesnt even show me what the cust chose for shipping"; (3) "how does my team process these orders? i click manage order and it shows me nothing just the status it needs to show me what i need"; (4) "did we send my team a email saying they got a order??". The order: Destiny Pugh / tiny4295@gmail.com / $26 / 1x Ember Warrior Graphic Tee XL / Powder Springs GA.
